**Decision**: Answer Units Generation questions Q1-Q3

---

## Question Answered
**Timestamp**: 2026-07-15T17:12:28Z
**Event**: QUESTION_ANSWERED
**Stage**: units-generation
**Details**: Q1: A. Vertical capability slices aligned to owned deployables and contracts (Recommended); Q2: A. Seven independently testable units covering contracts, Charge pricing, Booking command flow, CMM event flow, Booking projection, Booking UI/BFF, and live acceptance (Recommended); Q3: A. Preserve all valid parallel branches and record only hard compile-time, contract, or runtime-proof dependencies (Recommended)

---

## Decision Recorded
**Timestamp**: 2026-07-15T17:12:28Z
**Event**: DECISION_RECORDED
**Stage**: units-generation
**Decision**: Answer Units Generation questions Q4-Q5
**Options**: Dedicated contract-foundation unit,Service-owned copies,Acceptance-owned contracts,Runtime-only serializers;Hybrid deployables,Monolithic journey,Contract service,Embedded Booking UI

---

## Question Answered
**Timestamp**: 2026-07-15T17:13:01Z
**Event**: QUESTION_ANSWERED
**Stage**: units-generation
**Details**: Q4: A. A dedicated contract-foundation unit owns canonical Avro, AsyncAPI, OpenAPI, Pact, examples, catalog, and compatibility proof; service units consume it (Recommended); Q5: A. Hybrid: service capabilities remain independently deployable, Booking UI/BFF deploys as its app, contracts are shared build assets, and acceptance is evidence-only (Recommended)

## Question Answered
**Timestamp**: 2026-07-15T19:58:16Z
**Event**: QUESTION_ANSWERED
**Stage**: delivery-planning
**Details**: Q1: A. Hybrid walking-skeleton-first and risk-first: gate the real bidirectional Kafka journey first, then resilience, then release acceptance (Recommended); Q2: A. Use transparent ordinal value/risk/size comparison, not invented numeric precision, because the DAG and mandatory skeleton constrain the path (Recommended); Q3: A. Three Bolts: B01 bundles U01-U05 for the gated real round trip, B02 contains U06 resilience, and B03 contains U07 release acceptance (Recommended)

---

## Decision Recorded
**Timestamp**: 2026-07-15T19:58:16Z
**Event**: DECISION_RECORDED
**Stage**: delivery-planning
**Decision**: Answer Delivery Planning questions Q4-Q6
**Options**: Strictly sequential,Parallel all,Parallel by service,Ad hoc;Controlled local readiness,AWS/production,Assume readiness,External team;Integration/migration risks,UI styling,Performance first,Broader scope

---

## Question Answered
**Timestamp**: 2026-07-15T19:59:07Z
**Event**: QUESTION_ANSWERED
**Stage**: delivery-planning
**Details**: Q4: A. Strictly sequential through one stream-aligned mob; the hard DAG has one chain and shared files/runtime make parallel Bolt work unsafe (Recommended); Q5: A. No external team dependency; preflight local Docker/images/disk/ports, seeded Reference/Agreement data, and user gate availability as controlled readiness items (Recommended); Q6: A. Canonical schema rebaseline, Booking migration preservation, real Charge contract, two Kafka consumers, transaction/dedupe boundaries, and minimal browser-visible round trip (Recommended)

## Decision Recorded
**Timestamp**: 2026-07-15T20:09:26Z
**Event**: DECISION_RECORDED
**Stage**: functional-design
**Decision**: Answer U01 Functional Design questions Q1-Q3
**Options**: Additive upcast/backfill,Drop/recreate,Attributes authority,Reject legacy;Atomic create receipt,Separate writes,New retry draft,Browser-only;Server pages plus focused clients,Client workbench,Browser direct calls,Static mocks

---

## Question Answered
**Timestamp**: 2026-07-15T20:10:13Z
**Event**: QUESTION_ANSWERED
**Stage**: functional-design
**Details**: Q1: A. Additive V2 columns/tables plus a deterministic repository upcaster for legacy snapshots, followed by an idempotent backfill that rewrites canonical snapshots while preserving IDs, numbers, status, revision, lifecycle, and pricing data (Recommended); Q2: A. One transactional application boundary persists the canonical draft, command receipt/idempotency key plus request hash, and audit; same key/hash returns the existing draft and a different hash returns conflict (Recommended); Q3: A. Server pages/BFF own loading, backend URL, identity/correlation, and error normalization; focused client components own form input, focus, table interaction, and later polling, with no fallback records (Recommended)

---

## Decision Recorded
**Timestamp**: 2026-07-15T20:31:16Z
**Event**: DECISION_RECORDED
**Stage**: functional-design
**Decision**: Answer U02 Functional Design questions Q1-Q3
**Options**: Typed aggregate result,Fail-fast booleans,Outage as invalid,Copy records;Validation blocked state,Draft no reason,Generic exception,Delete draft;Route coherence,Any voyage,Partial validation,Trust browser

---

## Question Answered
**Timestamp**: 2026-07-15T20:31:56Z
**Event**: QUESTION_ANSWERED
**Stage**: functional-design
**Details**: Q1: A. Replace boolean lookups with one typed validation result containing all field outcomes (ACTIVE, INACTIVE, NOT_FOUND, MISMATCH) and a distinct provider-unavailable error; evaluate every required reference before returning (Recommended); Q2: A. Persist explicit VALIDATION_BLOCKED status plus the latest field-addressable validation result; successful retry replaces it with VALIDATED, while provider outage leaves business state unchanged (Recommended); Q3: A. The active voyage's canonical origin/destination reference IDs must match the selected leg's load/discharge locations, in addition to active customer, both locations, voyage, and equipment type (Recommended)

---
